        assert resume_child(db, run_id=run_id, **contract) == 'resumed'
        assert resume_child(db, run_id=run_id, **contract) == 'already_resumed'
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        events = db.scalars(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.subject_type == 'model_quota_pool')).all()
        assert len(events) == 2
        assert {e.action for e in events} == {'pacing_maintenance_pause', 'pacing_maintenance_resume'}
        assert all(e.event_key.startswith(f'batch-pacing:pool:{pool_id}:baseline:') for e in events)


def test_mysql_release_drain_resume_preserves_immutable_facts(mysql_incident):
    from app.baseline_release_drain import drain_child, resume_child
    from app.native_baseline_incident import resume_baseline_incident, _facts
    sessions, contract, report = mysql_incident
    with sessions() as db:
        resume_baseline_incident(db, **contract, limit=25)
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        facts = {run.id:_facts(db, run) for run in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun))}
        fees = {row.id:(row.status,row.estimated_cost_micros,row.actual_cost_micros)
                for row in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))}
        for run_id in report['recoverable_run_ids']:
            assert drain_child(db, run_id=run_id, **contract) == 'paused'
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        for run_id in report['recoverable_run_ids']:
            assert resume_child(db, run_id=run_id, **contract) == 'resumed'
        assert {run.id:_facts(db, run) for run in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun))} == facts
        assert {row.id:(row.status,row.estimated_cost_micros,row.actual_cost_micros)
                for row in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))} == fees
        db.commit()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_rule_diagnostics.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from app.baseline_rule_diagnostics import (
    build_rule_diagnostics,
    declared_rules,
)


def _track(dimensions):
    return {
        "sub_category_key": "functional_model",
        "common_group": {"schema_definition": {"dimensions": dimensions}},
    }


def _bundle(dimensions, *, level_scale=None, redline=None):
    return {
        "candidate_revision_id": 26,
        "config_revision": 9,
        "contract": {
            "level_scale": level_scale
            or {
                "levels": [
                    {"level": "L1", "min_score": 80, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L2", "min_score": 61, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L3", "min_score": 41, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L4", "min_score": 0, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L5", "enabled": False},
                ]
            },
            "redline_policy": redline
            or {"enabled": True, "hit_level": "L3", "hit_score_cap": 60, "rules": [{"key": "cg"}]},
        },
        "subcategory_dimensions": {"functional_model": _track(dimensions)},
    }


DIMENSIONS = [
    {
        "key": "model_detail",
        "label": "模型细节",
        "deduction_rules": [
            {"rule_id": "minor_defect", "description": "微瑕", "deduction": 30},
            {"rule_id": "major_defect", "description": "明显缺陷", "deduction": 60},
        ],
        "bonus_rules": [{"rule_id": "extra_polish", "description": "额外精修", "bonus": 5}],
    },
    {
        "key": "lighting",
        "label": "光照",
        "deduction_rules": [{"rule_id": "flat_light", "description": "平光", "deduction": 30}],
    },
]


def _item(score, level, *, hits=None, bonus=None, caps=None):
    dimensions = {}
    for dimension_key, rule_ids in (hits or {}).items():
        dimensions.setdefault(dimension_key, {})["hit_rules"] = [
            {"rule_id": rule_id} for rule_id in rule_ids
        ]
    for dimension_key, rule_ids in (bonus or {}).items():
        dimensions.setdefault(dimension_key, {})["hit_bonus_rules"] = [
            {"rule_id": rule_id} for rule_id in rule_ids
        ]
    if not dimensions:
        dimensions = {"model_detail": {"hit_rules": [], "hit_bonus_rules": []}}
    return {
        "predicted_level": level,
        "authoritative_score": score,
        "cap_reasons": caps or [],
        "scoring": {"dimension_deduction_output": {"dimensions": dimensions}},
    }


def test_declared_rules_flattens_deduction_and_bonus_rules():
    rules = declared_rules({"functional_model": _track(DIMENSIONS)})

    assert [(r["dimension_key"], r["rule_id"], r["kind"]) for r in rules] == [
        ("lighting", "flat_light", "deduction"),
        ("model_detail", "extra_polish", "bonus"),
        ("model_detail", "major_defect", "deduction"),
        ("model_detail", "minor_defect", "deduction"),
    ]
    assert rules[3]["points"] == 30
    assert rules[1]["points"] == 5


def test_same_rule_across_tracks_is_counted_once_and_lists_both_tracks():
    dimensions = {
        "functional_model": _track(DIMENSIONS),
        "soft_furnishing": _track(DIMENSIONS),
    }

    rules = declared_rules(dimensions)

    assert len(rules) == 4
    assert rules[0]["tracks"] == ["functional_model", "soft_furnishing"]


def test_inert_rule_layer_is_reported_when_no_rule_ever_fires():
    """规则层空转必须被明确标出。

    这正是把所有图判成 L1 的真实成因：模型一条规则都不报，于是调高扣分、
    下调红线阈值全部空转。看不到这一点，运营只会继续调错地方。
    """
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=_bundle(DIMENSIONS),
        item_snapshots=[_item(88, "L1") for _ in range(5)],
    )

    assert report["rule_layer_inert"] is True
    assert report["scored_item_count"] == 5
    assert report["items_without_rule_hits"] == 5
    assert report["declared_rule_count"] == 4
    assert report["never_hit_rule_count"] == 4
    assert report["items_with_score_caps"] == 0
    # 无扣分时分数落在哪一档 —— 解释了为什么全是 L1
    assert report["unpenalised_level"] == "L1"
    assert report["level_distribution"] == {"L1": 5}


def test_partial_hits_are_counted_per_rule_and_layer_is_not_inert():
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=_bundle(DIMENSIONS),
        item_snapshots=[
            _item(70, "L2", hits={"model_detail": ["minor_defect"]}),
            _item(70, "L2", hits={"model_detail": ["minor_defect"]}),
            _item(40, "L4", hits={"lighting": ["flat_light"]}),
            _item(88, "L1"),
        ],
    )

    assert report["rule_layer_inert"] is False
    assert report["items_without_rule_hits"] == 1
    by_rule = {(r["dimension_key"], r["rule_id"]): r["hit_count"] for r in report["rules"]}
    assert by_rule[("model_detail", "minor_defect")] == 2
    assert by_rule[("lighting", "flat_light")] == 1
    assert by_rule[("model_detail", "major_defect")] == 0
    assert report["never_hit_rule_count"] == 2  # major_defect 与 extra_polish
    assert report["unpenalised_level"] == "L1"


def test_bonus_hits_count_and_undeclared_hits_are_surfaced():
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=_bundle(DIMENSIONS),
        item_snapshots=[
            _item(92, "L1", bonus={"model_detail": ["extra_polish"]}),
            _item(60, "L3", hits={"model_detail": ["ghost_rule"]}),
        ],
    )

    by_rule = {(r["dimension_key"], r["rule_id"]): r["hit_count"] for r in report["rules"]}
    assert by_rule[("model_detail", "extra_polish")] == 1
    # 模型报了合同未声明的规则 id —— 必须暴露而非静默忽略
    assert report["undeclared_hits"] == [
        {"dimension_key": "model_detail", "rule_id": "ghost_rule", "hit_count": 1}
    ]


def test_score_buckets_and_redline_summary_are_included():
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=_bundle(DIMENSIONS),
        item_snapshots=[_item(88, "L1"), _item(84, "L1"), _item(52, "L3")],
    )

    assert report["score_buckets"] == {"50-59": 1, "80-89": 2}
    assert report["redline_policy"] == {
        "enabled": True,
        "hit_level": "L3",
        "hit_score_cap": 60,
    }
    assert report["redline_rule_count"] == 1
    assert "rules" not in report["redline_policy"]


def test_empty_run_is_not_reported_as_inert():
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=_bundle(DIMENSIONS), item_snapshots=[]
    )

    assert report["rule_layer_inert"] is False
    assert report["scored_item_count"] == 0
    assert report["unpenalised_level"] is None


def test_unscored_items_still_contribute_level_and_cap_counts():
    """尚未产出维度输出的条目不算入规则统计，但等级与上限计数仍要如实反映。"""
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=_bundle(DIMENSIONS),
        item_snapshots=[
            {"predicted_level": "L1", "authoritative_score": 88, "cap_reasons": ["cg"]},
            _item(70, "L2", hits={"lighting": ["flat_light"]}),
        ],
    )

    assert report["scored_item_count"] == 1
    assert report["items_with_score_caps"] == 1
    assert report["level_distribution"] == {"L1": 1, "L2": 1}


def test_unpenalised_level_uses_median_so_one_outlier_does_not_skew_it():
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=_bundle(DIMENSIONS),
        item_snapshots=[_item(88, "L1"), _item(85, "L1"), _item(10, "L4")],
    )

    # 中位数 85 → L1；若用均值 (61) 会误报成 L2
    assert report["unpenalised_level"] == "L1"


def test_disabled_levels_are_ignored_when_mapping_scores():
    scale = {
        "levels": [
            {"level": "L1", "min_score": 80, "enabled": True},
            {"level": "L5", "min_score": 95, "enabled": False},
        ]
    }
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=_bundle(DIMENSIONS, level_scale=scale),
        item_snapshots=[_item(97, "L1")],
    )

    assert report["unpenalised_level"] == "L1"


def test_unpenalised_level_follows_legacy_thresholds_like_runtime():
    """冻结合同只带 level_thresholds 时，诊断与聚合器用同一张阈值表。"""
    bundle = _bundle(DIMENSIONS)
    contract = bundle["contract"]
    contract.pop("level_scale")
    contract["level_thresholds"] = [
        {"min_score": 90, "level": "L1"},
        {"min_score": 80, "level": "L2"},
        {"min_score": 60, "level": "L3"},
        {"min_score": 0, "level": "L4"},
    ]
    report = build_rule_diagnostics(
        frozen_bundle=bundle,
        item_snapshots=[_item(85, "L2")],
    )

    assert report["unpenalised_level"] == "L2"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_set_detail_filter.py =====
"""基准集详情：按素材名筛选 + 分页，供 2000+ 张黄金集的真值管理面板定位单张。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.main import baseline_set_detail
from test_native_baseline_regression import _add_level_truth_item, _baseline


def test_detail_filters_by_asset_name_and_paginates_filtered_total(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'detail-filter.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)  # 自带 native-stock.jpg
            created = {
                suffix: _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix=suffix)
                for suffix in ("好_100", "好_101", "中差_200")
            }
            db.flush()

            full = baseline_set_detail(baseline.id, _user=None, db=db)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            assert full["summary"]["item_count"] == 4
            assert full["pagination"]["total"] == 4
            assert full["pagination"]["q"] is None

            hit = baseline_set_detail(baseline.id, q="  好_  ", _user=None, db=db)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            assert [item["asset_id"] for item in hit["items"]] == [
                created["好_100"].asset_id,
                created["好_101"].asset_id,
            ]
            assert hit["pagination"]["total"] == 2
            assert hit["pagination"]["q"] == "好_"
            # 汇总里的张数仍是整集，不随筛选变
            assert hit["summary"]["item_count"] == 4

            page2 = baseline_set_detail(
                baseline.id, q="好_", limit=1, offset=1, _user=None, db=db  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            )
            assert [item["asset_id"] for item in page2["items"]] == [created["好_101"].asset_id]
            assert page2["pagination"] == {"offset": 1, "limit": 1, "total": 2, "q": "好_"}

            miss = baseline_set_detail(baseline.id, q="不存在", _user=None, db=db)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            assert miss["items"] == [] and miss["pagination"]["total"] == 0

            compact = baseline_set_detail(baseline.id, include_items=False, q="好_", _user=None, db=db)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            assert compact["items"] == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_summary_memory.py =====
"""Summary reads must not retain every historical result in ORM relationships."""
import importlib
import json

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, inspect
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineRegressionItem


def test_runtime_summary_streams_frozen_status_without_loading_all_items(monkeypatch):
    main = importlib.import_module("app.main")
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_baseline_run_selection", lambda run: {"workflow": {"id": 1}})
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_baseline_model_cost_summary", lambda run: None)
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    BaselineRegressionRun.__table__.create(engine)
    BaselineRegressionItem.__table__.create(engine)
    snapshots = [json.dumps({"workflow_runtime": {"status": state},
                             "evidence": "x" * 100_000})
                 for state in ("succeeded", "failed", "canceled", "running", "succeeded")]
    snapshots += ["{}", "broken json"]
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(BaselineRegressionRun(id=1, baseline_set_id=1, sequence_no=1,
            category_key="inspiration_image", strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
            baseline_set_fingerprint="test", total=len(snapshots)))
        db.add_all([BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=1, baseline_set_item_id=i,
            asset_id=i, expected_level="L3", result_snapshot_json=s)
            for i, s in enumerate(snapshots, 1)])
        db.commit()
    statements = []
    event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute",
                 lambda conn, cursor, statement, parameters, context, many: statements.append(statement))
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, 1)
        assert main._baseline_run_summary(run)["workflow_runtime"] == {
            "selected": True, "total": 7,
            "succeeded": 2, "failed": 1, "canceled": 1, "pending": 3}
        assert "items" in inspect(run).unloaded
        assert not any(isinstance(obj, BaselineRegressionItem) for obj in db.identity_map.values())
        item_queries = [s for s in statements if "FROM baseline_regression_items" in s]
        assert len(item_queries) == 1
        assert "baseline_regression_items.id," not in item_queries[0]
        assert "baseline_regression_items.result_snapshot_json" in item_queries[0]
    engine.dispose()


def test_runtime_summary_keeps_detached_fixture_semantics():
    main = importlib.import_module("app.main")
    run = BaselineRegressionRun()
    run.items = [BaselineRegressionItem(result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
        {"workflow_runtime": {"status": "succeeded"}}))]
    assert main._baseline_workflow_status_counts(run) == {
        "succeeded": 1, "failed": 0, "canceled": 0, "pending": 0}


def test_runtime_summary_keeps_unflushed_relationship_without_querying():
    main = importlib.import_module("app.main")
    # An attached, unflushed run has no DB identity. It must not query run_id=NULL
    # or autoflush a partially constructed fixture just to summarize it.
    with Session() as db:
        run = BaselineRegressionRun()
        run.items = [BaselineRegressionItem(result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {"workflow_runtime": {"status": "failed"}}))]
        db.add(run)
        assert main._baseline_workflow_status_counts(run) == {
            "succeeded": 0, "failed": 1, "canceled": 0, "pending": 0}
        assert inspect(run).pending


def test_runtime_summary_prefers_counts_persisted_in_metrics_without_touching_items(monkeypatch):
    """09-30：/api/baseline-sets 每次为分类下所有 run 流式解析结果快照算状态计数（每分钟 168 次 × 233 ms）。
    compute_truth_metrics 现在把 workflow_status_counts 写进 metrics_json，汇总直接读，不再查 items。"""
    main = importlib.import_module("app.main")
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_baseline_run_selection", lambda run: {"workflow": {"id": 1}})
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_baseline_model_cost_summary", lambda run: None)
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    BaselineRegressionRun.__table__.create(engine)
    BaselineRegressionItem.__table__.create(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(BaselineRegressionRun(id=1, baseline_set_id=1, sequence_no=1, category_key="inspiration_image",
            strategy_snapshot_json="{}", baseline_set_fingerprint="test", total=3,
            metrics_json=json.dumps({"workflow_status_counts": {"succeeded": 2, "failed": 0, "canceled": 0, "pending": 1}})))
        db.add_all([BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=1, baseline_set_item_id=i, asset_id=i, expected_level="L3",
            result_snapshot_json=json.dumps({"workflow_runtime": {"status": "failed"}})) for i in (1, 2, 3)])
        db.commit()
    statements = []
    event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute",
                 lambda conn, cursor, statement, parameters, context, many: statements.append(statement))
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, 1)
        assert main._baseline_run_summary(run)["workflow_runtime"] == {
            "selected": True, "total": 3, "succeeded": 2, "failed": 0, "canceled": 0, "pending": 1}
        assert not any("FROM baseline_regression_items" in s for s in statements)
        # 缺键或键损坏才回退到流式解析
        run.metrics_json = json.dumps({"workflow_status_counts": {"succeeded": "x"}})
        assert main._baseline_workflow_status_counts(run) == {"succeeded": 0, "failed": 3, "canceled": 0, "pending": 0}
    engine.dispose()


def test_compute_truth_metrics_carries_workflow_status_counts():
    from app.native_baseline_regression import compute_truth_metrics, _metric_row
    rows = [{"status": "completed", "workflow_status": "succeeded", "expected_result": None, "predicted_result": None},
            {"status": "failed", "workflow_status": "canceled", "expected_result": None, "predicted_result": None},
            {"status": "queued", "expected_result": None, "predicted_result": None}]
    assert compute_truth_metrics(rows)["workflow_status_counts"] == {"succeeded": 1, "failed": 0, "canceled": 1, "pending": 1}
    item = BaselineRegressionItem(status="completed", expected_level="L3", expected_result_json="{}",
        result_snapshot_json=json.dumps({"workflow_runtime": {"status": "succeeded"}, "normalized_result": {}}))
    assert _metric_row(item)["workflow_status"] == "succeeded"


def test_backfill_script_writes_counts_per_run_under_lock_and_is_idempotent(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    """评审 R2：回填改为每 run 独立事务、先锁父行再重读，只补缺失键，不动其它指标；重跑全部跳过。"""
    import runpy, sys
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
    import app.database as database
    main = importlib.import_module("app.main")
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_baseline_run_selection", lambda run: {"workflow": {"id": 1}})
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///" + str(tmp_path / "bf.sqlite"))
    BaselineRegressionRun.__table__.create(engine)
    BaselineRegressionItem.__table__.create(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(BaselineRegressionRun(id=1, baseline_set_id=1, sequence_no=1, category_key="inspiration_image",
            strategy_snapshot_json="{}", baseline_set_fingerprint="t", total=2, completed=2,
            metrics_json=json.dumps({"completed": 2, "pending": 0, "exact_accuracy": 0.5})))
        db.add_all([BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=1, baseline_set_item_id=i, asset_id=i, expected_level="L3",
            result_snapshot_json=json.dumps({"workflow_runtime": {"status": s}})) for i, s in ((1, "succeeded"), (2, "failed"))])
        db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(database, "SessionLocal", sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False))
    script = str(__import__("pathlib").Path(main.__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "backfill-baseline-workflow-status-counts.py")
    for expected in ('"written": 1', '"already": 1'):
        monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", [script])
        try:
            runpy.run_path(script, run_name="__main__")
        except SystemExit as exit_:
            assert exit_.code == 0
    with Session(engine) as db:
        metrics = json.loads(db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, 1).metrics_json)
    assert metrics["workflow_status_counts"] == {"succeeded": 1, "failed": 1, "canceled": 0, "pending": 0}
    assert metrics["completed"] == 2 and metrics["exact_accuracy"] == 0.5
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_summary_projection.py =====
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import Text, column, create_engine, literal, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.baseline_summary_projection import summary_json_projection


@pytest.mark.parametrize("selected", [None, 0, "中文", [], {"status": "succeeded", "flag": False}])
def test_history_projection_preserves_json_types_and_excludes_heavy_evidence(selected):
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        raw = json.dumps({"workflow_runtime": selected, "model_output": "x" * 500_000})
        projected = db.scalar(select(summary_json_projection(db, literal(raw), "workflow_runtime")))
        assert json.loads(projected) == {"workflow_runtime": selected}
        assert len(projected) < 100
    engine.dispose()


def test_sqlite_scalar_boolean_cannot_become_valid_status_or_cost_evidence():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        value = db.scalar(select(summary_json_projection(
            db, literal('{"workflow_runtime":false}'), "workflow_runtime")))
        assert not isinstance(json.loads(value)["workflow_runtime"], dict)
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw", [None, "", "broken json", "{}", "[]"])
def test_history_projection_retains_missing_or_invalid_json_as_no_status(raw):
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        value = db.scalar(select(summary_json_projection(db, literal(raw), "workflow_runtime")))
        assert json.loads(value).get("workflow_runtime") is None
    engine.dispose()


def test_pricing_projection_keeps_both_original_evidence_objects():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    expected = {"reservation": {"pricing_tier_key": "0-32000"},
                "pricing_settlement": {"actual_cost_micros": 0, "cache_hit": False}}
    with Session(engine) as db:
        raw = json.dumps({**expected, "response": "x" * 500_000})
        projected = db.scalar(select(summary_json_projection(db, literal(raw), *expected)))
        assert json.loads(projected) == expected
        assert len(projected) < 200
    engine.dispose()


def test_other_database_dialects_keep_the_original_column():
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    db = SimpleNamespace(get_bind=lambda: SimpleNamespace(dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="postgresql")))
    source = column("evidence_json", Text)
    assert summary_json_projection(db, source, "reservation") is source


def test_set_summary_reuses_the_same_request_latest_run_summary(monkeypatch):
    from app import main
    from app.models import BaselineSet, BaselineRegressionRun
    baseline = BaselineSet(id=7, name="test", category_key="model_3d_su")
    baseline.items = []
    baseline.runs = [BaselineRegressionRun(id=10), BaselineRegressionRun(id=11)]
    saved = {"id": 11, "model_cost": {"actual_cost_known": False}}
    def unexpected(_run):
        raise AssertionError("the latest run must not scan its frozen evidence twice")
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_baseline_run_summary", unexpected)
    assert main._baseline_set_summary(baseline, run_summaries={11: saved})["latest_run"] == saved

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_truth_edit_api.py =====
"""运营不经回归、直接给基准集条目追加人工真值。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import contextmanager
from typing import Iterator

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, BaselineTruthRevision, User


@contextmanager
def _context() -> Iterator[dict[str, object]]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        user = User(
            username="ops",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="运营",
            is_admin=True,
            role="admin",
        )
        level_asset = Asset(
            original_name="level.jpg", stored_name="level.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=10, sha256="1" * 64, category_key="inspiration_image",
        )
        kg_asset = Asset(
            original_name="国内_整体_1_2.jpg", stored_name="kg.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=10, sha256="2" * 64, category_key="knowledge_graph",
        )
        level_set = BaselineSet(
            category_key="inspiration_image", name="等级集", description="",
            default_expected_level="L2", fingerprint="a" * 64, created_by="ops",
        )
        kg_set = BaselineSet(
            category_key="knowledge_graph", name="知识图谱集", description="",
            default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="b" * 64, created_by="ops",
        )
        db.add_all([user, level_asset, kg_asset, level_set, kg_set])
        db.flush()
        level_item = BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=level_set.id, asset_id=level_asset.id, expected_level="L2",
            expected_result_json=None, truth_contract_key=None,
            asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json({"asset_id": level_asset.id, "sha256": level_asset.sha256}),
        )
        kg_item = BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=kg_set.id, asset_id=kg_asset.id, expected_level=None,
            expected_result_json=canonical_json({
                "ll_id": "1", "is_single": 0, "space": "", "object": "", "style": "",
                "material": "", "structural_features": "", "architectural_element": "",
                "soft_decoration": "", "hard_decoration": "", "color": "", "form": "", "structure": "",
            }),
            truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
            asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json({
                "asset_id": kg_asset.id, "sha256": kg_asset.sha256,
                "workflow_input": {"source": "domestic", "is_single": 0, "ll_id": "1", "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1"},
            }),
        )
        db.add_all([level_item, kg_item])
        db.commit()
        ids = {
            "level_set": level_set.id, "level_item": level_item.id,
            "kg_set": kg_set.id, "kg_item": kg_item.id,
        }

    def override_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        yield {"client": TestClient(app), "sessions": sessions, **ids}
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_level_truth_can_be_edited_without_a_regression_run() -> None:
    with _context() as fx:
        client = fx["client"]
        url = f"/api/baseline-sets/{fx['level_set']}/items/{fx['level_item']}/truth"
        res = client.post(url, json={"edit_key": "e1", "reason": "复核后应为 L1", "expected_level": "L1"})
        assert res.status_code == 200, res.text
        body = res.json()
        assert body["expected_level"] == "L1"
        assert body["truth_source"] == "revision"
        assert body["truth_revision"] == 1
        assert body["original_truth"]["expected_level"] == "L2"  # 原始导入真值不被改写

        # 同一 edit_key 重放：幂等，不再追加
        again = client.post(url, json={"edit_key": "e1", "reason": "复核后应为 L1", "expected_level": "L1"})
        assert again.status_code == 200 and again.json()["truth_revision"] == 1
        with fx["sessions"]() as db:
            assert len(db.scalars(select(BaselineTruthRevision)).all()) == 1


def test_structured_truth_fields_can_be_edited_and_are_contract_validated() -> None:
    with _context() as fx:
        client = fx["client"]
        url = f"/api/baseline-sets/{fx['kg_set']}/items/{fx['kg_item']}/truth"
        good = {
            "ll_id": "1", "is_single": 0, "space": "客厅", "object": "沙发_0.7,茶几_0.5",
            "style": "现代简约", "material": "木材", "structural_features": "",
            "architectural_element": "", "soft_decoration": "地毯", "hard_decoration": "",
            "color": "米白色", "form": "几何", "structure": "组合",
        }
        res = client.post(url, json={
            "edit_key": "k1", "reason": "按图标注", "expected_result": good,
            "truth_contract_key": "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
        })
        assert res.status_code == 200, res.text
        assert res.json()["expected_result"]["space"] == "客厅"
        assert res.json()["pending_truth_field_count"] < 12

        # 违反合同（color 多值）必须被拒，且不落库
        bad = {**good, "color": "米白色,黑色"}
        rej = client.post(url, json={
            "edit_key": "k2", "reason": "错误示例", "expected_result": bad,
            "truth_contract_key": "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
        })
        assert rej.status_code == 422, rej.text
        assert rej.json()["detail"]["code"] == "kg_output_value_invalid"
        with fx["sessions"]() as db:
            assert len(db.scalars(select(BaselineTruthRevision)).all()) == 1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_truth_import.py =====
"""回填运营改过的导出表：只认真值列、留空不改、必须先预览。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.baseline_truth_import import (
    BaselineTruthImportError,
    parse_is_single_cell,
    parse_truth_sheet,
    preview_payload,
)
from test_p0e_safe_import import _write_xlsx

OVERSEAS = "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1"
DOMESTIC = "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.single.v1"

HEAD = [
    "条目ID", "图片", "素材", "路线", "状态", "不一致字段数",
    "海外素材 ID（模型）", "海外素材 ID（真值）",
    "整体/单体（模型）", "整体/单体（真值）",
    "英文标题（模型）", "英文标题（真值）",
    "主要材质（模型）", "主要材质（真值）",
]


def _sheet(tmp_path, rows, name="edited.xlsx"):
    path = tmp_path / name
    _write_xlsx(path, [HEAD, *rows])
    return path


CURRENT = {
    11: {"res_id": "6938137", "is_single": 1, "title": "Old Title", "material": ""},
    12: {"res_id": "4365007", "is_single": 1, "title": "Sofa", "material": "fabric"},
}


def test_only_truth_columns_are_read(tmp_path) -> None:
    """模型列是历史事实，改了也必须忽略。"""

    path = _sheet(tmp_path, [[
        11, "", "a.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
        "TAMPERED", "6938137",          # 模型列被改 → 忽略
        "9 乱填", "1 整体",              # 模型列被改 → 忽略
        "TAMPERED", "New Title",        # 真值改了 → 记一笔
        "TAMPERED", "",                 # 真值留空 → 不改
    ]])
    parsed = parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    assert [(c.field, c.after) for c in parsed.changes] == [("title", "New Title")]


def test_blank_cell_means_no_change_not_clear(tmp_path) -> None:
    """漏填一格不该把已有真值抹掉。"""

    path = _sheet(tmp_path, [[
        12, "", "b.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
        "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "",
    ]])
    parsed = parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    assert parsed.changes == []
    assert parsed.unchanged_rows == 1


def test_explicit_marker_clears_the_value(tmp_path) -> None:
    """要清空得显式填 -，这样误操作和有意为之能分开。"""

    path = _sheet(tmp_path, [[
        12, "", "b.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
        "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "-",
    ]])
    parsed = parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    assert [(c.field, c.before, c.after) for c in parsed.changes] == [
        ("material", "fabric", "")
    ]


def test_exported_placeholders_round_trip_without_changes(tmp_path) -> None:
    """原样传回导出的表不该凭空产生修订。"""

    path = _sheet(tmp_path, [[
        11, "", "a.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
        "6938137", "6938137", "1 整体", "1 整体",
        "Old Title", "Old Title", "", "（未填）",
    ]])
    parsed = parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    assert parsed.changes == []
    assert parsed.unchanged_rows == 1


def test_is_single_cell_is_parsed_back_to_its_code(tmp_path) -> None:
    assert parse_is_single_cell("2 单体") == 2
    assert parse_is_single_cell("1 整体（原值 0）") == 1
    assert parse_is_single_cell("整体") is None

    path = _sheet(tmp_path, [[
        11, "", "a.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
        "", "", "", "2 单体", "", "", "", "",
    ]])
    parsed = parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    assert [(c.field, c.before, c.after) for c in parsed.changes] == [
        ("is_single", 1, 2)
    ]


def test_unrecognisable_is_single_skips_the_whole_row(tmp_path) -> None:
    """一格填错就跳过整行，不能只写一半。"""

    path = _sheet(tmp_path, [[
        11, "", "a.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
        "", "", "", "整体", "", "New Title", "", "",
    ]])
    parsed = parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    assert parsed.changes == []
    assert "整体/单体" in parsed.skipped[0]["reason"]


def test_rows_outside_this_run_are_skipped(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = _sheet(tmp_path, [[
        999, "", "x.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
        "", "", "", "", "", "Hacked", "", "",
    ]])
    parsed = parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    assert parsed.changes == []
    assert "不属于本轮" in parsed.skipped[0]["reason"]


def test_sheet_without_id_column_is_refused(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "noid.xlsx"
    _write_xlsx(path, [["素材", "英文标题（真值）"], ["a.jpg", "X"]])
    with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthImportError) as err:
        parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    assert err.value.code == "baseline_truth_import_id_column_missing"


def test_preview_groups_changes_by_item_and_never_writes(tmp_path) -> None:
    path = _sheet(tmp_path, [
        [11, "", "a.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
         "", "", "", "2 单体", "", "New Title", "", ""],
        [12, "", "b.jpg", "海外·整体", "completed", "未比对",
         "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "-"],
    ])
    parsed = parse_truth_sheet(path, current_truth=CURRENT, contract_keys=[OVERSEAS])
    payload = preview_payload(parsed)
    assert payload["writes_business_database"] is False
    assert payload["summary"] == {
        "changed_items": 2, "changed_fields": 3,
        "unchanged_rows": 0, "skipped_rows": 0,
    }
    first = payload["items"][0]
    assert first["item_id"] == 11
    assert {c["field"] for c in first["changes"]} == {"is_single", "title"}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_truth_import_apply.py =====
"""回填真值落库：一条失败不能把已经写好的抹掉。

2026-09-17 正式服实证：基准集 19 的回填回执写着「applied_items=147、failed_items=248」，
而 baseline_truth_revisions 当天一条都没多。原因是 apply_changes 逐条循环里失败走了
db.rollback()——那是整事务回滚，把前面 flush 过的修订全带走了，只有最后一次失败之后的
几条能活到 commit。回执里的计数只是列表长度，审计又在函数返回后才写，所以界面显示成功、
库里是空的，运营完全看不出来。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.baseline_truth_import import ParsedTruthSheet, TruthChange, apply_changes
from app.database import Base
from app.models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, BaselineTruthRevision

CONTRACT = "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
# 合同要求 13 个字段全填，缺一个就整条不合格——夹具必须给全，否则测的就不是回滚了。
FIELDS = ("space", "object", "style", "material", "structural_features", "architectural_element",
          "soft_decoration", "hard_decoration", "color", "form", "structure")


def _truth(index: int) -> dict:
    # 整体链路的 object 有权重规则（0.7/0.5/0.3），随手填字符串过不了合同。
    base = {name: "unset" for name in FIELDS}
    base["object"] = "vase_0.7,plant_0.5"
    return {"ll_id": f"ll-{index}", "is_single": 0, **base}


def _session(tmp_path, name: str) -> Session:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / name}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return Session(engine)


def _items(db: Session, count: int) -> list[BaselineSetItem]:
    baseline = BaselineSet(
        category_key="knowledge_graph", name="回填落库测试集", description="",
        default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="f" * 64, created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    made: list[BaselineSetItem] = []
    for index in range(count):
        asset = Asset(
            original_name=f"kg-{index}.jpg", stored_name=f"kg-{index}.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=20, sha256=f"{index:064d}",
            category_key="knowledge_graph",
        )
        db.add(asset)
        db.flush()
        item = BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=asset.id, expected_level=None,
            expected_result_json=canonical_json(_truth(index)),
            truth_contract_key=CONTRACT,
            asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json({
                "asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256,
                "workflow_input": {"source": "domestic", "is_single": 0, "ll_id": f"ll-{index}"},
            }),
        )
        db.add(item)
        db.flush()
        made.append(item)
    return made


def _apply(db: Session, items, *, bad_index: int | None, edit_key: str) -> dict:
    """给每条改一个 style；bad_index 那条塞一个合同不认的字段名，必定失败。"""
    changes, contracts, current = [], {}, {}
    for position, item in enumerate(items):
        contracts[item.id] = CONTRACT
        current[item.id] = _truth(position)
        field = "字段不存在" if position == bad_index else "style"
        changes.append(TruthChange(
            row_number=position + 2, item_id=item.id, field=field,
            label=field, before="unset", after="modern simple",
        ))
    return apply_changes(
        db, ParsedTruthSheet(changes=changes), contracts=contracts,
        current_truth=current, actor="liangjx", edit_key=edit_key, reason="人工校验后回填",
    )


def test_a_failing_row_does_not_erase_the_rows_already_written(tmp_path) -> None:
    db = _session(tmp_path, "partial.db")
    try:
        items = _items(db, 3)
        # 中间那条失败：修好之前，第 1、3 条也会被整事务回滚一起抹掉。
        result = _apply(db, items, bad_index=1, edit_key="truth-import:1:aaa")
        db.commit()

        assert result["summary"]["applied_items"] == 2
        assert result["summary"]["failed_items"] == 1
        # 回执说写了几条，库里就必须有几条——这正是原先对不上的地方。
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(BaselineTruthRevision.id))) == 2
        written = set(db.scalars(select(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id)))
        assert written == {items[0].id, items[2].id}
    finally:
        db.close()


def test_a_failure_on_the_last_row_still_keeps_the_earlier_ones(tmp_path) -> None:
    """最坏的情况：最后一条失败。原先这一下会把整批都回滚掉，回执却照报成功。"""
    db = _session(tmp_path, "last-fails.db")
    try:
        items = _items(db, 3)
        result = _apply(db, items, bad_index=2, edit_key="truth-import:2:bbb")
        db.commit()
        assert result["summary"]["applied_items"] == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(BaselineTruthRevision.id))) == 2
    finally:
        db.close()


def test_every_failure_carries_a_reason_for_the_operator(tmp_path) -> None:
    db = _session(tmp_path, "reasons.db")
    try:
        items = _items(db, 2)
        result = _apply(db, items, bad_index=0, edit_key="truth-import:3:ccc")
        db.commit()
        assert len(result["failed"]) == 1
        failure = result["failed"][0]
        assert failure["item_id"] == items[0].id
        assert failure["reason"], "失败必须带原因，否则运营只能盲目重试"
    finally:
        db.close()


def test_all_rows_good_writes_all_of_them(tmp_path) -> None:
    db = _session(tmp_path, "all-good.db")
    try:
        items = _items(db, 3)
        result = _apply(db, items, bad_index=None, edit_key="truth-import:4:ddd")
        db.commit()
        assert result["summary"]["applied_items"] == 3 and result["summary"]["failed_items"] == 0
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(BaselineTruthRevision.id))) == 3
    finally:
        db.close()


def test_audit_summary_keeps_failure_reasons_and_item_ids():
    """审计不能只剩「failed_items: 248」；原因直方图和条目 id 要留下来。"""
    from app.baseline_truth_import import audit_summary
    result = {
        "summary": {"applied_items": 1, "failed_items": 3},
        "failed": [
            {"item_id": 11, "reason": "object 最多允许 3 个值"},
            {"item_id": 12, "reason": "object 最多允许 3 个值"},
            {"item_id": 13, "reason": "res_id 必须原样返回当前素材 ID"},
        ],
    }
    payload = audit_summary(result)
    assert payload["applied_items"] == 1 and payload["failed_items"] == 3
    assert payload["failed_reasons"] == [
        {"reason": "object 最多允许 3 个值", "count": 2},
        {"reason": "res_id 必须原样返回当前素材 ID", "count": 1},
    ]
    assert payload["failed_item_ids"] == [11, 12, 13]


def test_union_columns_are_filtered_per_item_contract(tmp_path) -> None:
    """导出表的真值列是本轮所有路线的并集；不属于本条目路线的列、身份字段与
    is_single 一律按「已忽略」记录，不能让整条失败（2026-09-18 run215 实证：
    248 条全失败，其中 200 条是海外条目的空 ll_id 列被当成清空）。"""
    db = _session(tmp_path, "union-columns.db")
    overseas = "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1"
    truth = {
        "res_id": "7898560", "is_single": 1, "title": "old", "space": "", "object": "vase_0.7",
        "style": "", "material": "", "structural_features": "", "architectural_element": "",
        "soft_decoration": "", "hard_decoration": "", "color": "", "form": "", "structure": "",
    }
    baseline = BaselineSet(
        category_key="knowledge_graph", name="并集列测试集", description="",
        default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="e" * 64, created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="kg-os.jpg", stored_name="kg-os.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=20, sha256="a" * 64, category_key="knowledge_graph",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    item = BaselineSetItem(
        baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=asset.id, expected_level=None,
        expected_result_json=canonical_json(truth), truth_contract_key=overseas,
        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json({
            "asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256,
            "workflow_input": {"source": "overseas", "is_single": 1, "res_id": "7898560"},
        }),
    )
    db.add(item)
    db.flush()

    changes = [
        TruthChange(row_number=2, item_id=item.id, field="title", label="英文标题", before="old", after="new"),
        # 海外条目的国内 ID 列留空 → 解析成「清空」，不该让整条失败
        TruthChange(row_number=2, item_id=item.id, field="ll_id", label="国内素材 ID", before=None, after=""),
        # 身份字段与路线字段都不是真值
        TruthChange(row_number=2, item_id=item.id, field="res_id", label="海外素材 ID", before="7898560", after="999"),
        TruthChange(row_number=2, item_id=item.id, field="is_single", label="整体/单体", before=1, after=0),
    ]
    result = apply_changes(
        db, ParsedTruthSheet(changes=changes),
        # 本轮同时有国内与海外两条路线，所以导出表带了两种身份列
        contracts={item.id: overseas, -1: "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"},
        current_truth={item.id: truth},
        actor="liangjx", edit_key="truth-import:9:union", reason="人工校验后回填",
    )
    assert result["summary"]["failed_items"] == 0, result["failed"]
    assert result["summary"]["applied_items"] == 1
    assert [a["fields"] for a in result["applied"]] == [["title"]]
    ignored = {row["field"]: row["reason"] for row in result["ignored"]}
    assert set(ignored) == {"ll_id", "res_id", "is_single"}
    assert "不属于本条目的路线" in ignored["ll_id"]
    assert "必须等于素材 ID" in ignored["res_id"]
    assert "整体/单体由素材路线决定" in ignored["is_single"]
    db.commit()
    revision = db.scalar(select(BaselineTruthRevision).where(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id == item.id))
    assert revision is not None
    written = json.loads(revision.expected_result_json)
    assert written["title"] == "new" and written["res_id"] == "7898560" and written["is_single"] == 1
    assert "ll_id" not in written
    db.close()


def test_a_field_outside_every_contract_still_fails(tmp_path) -> None:
    """并集列可以忽略，但列名被改坏、伪造字段仍必须失败——否则误操作被静默吞掉。"""
    from app.baseline_truth_import import ParsedTruthSheet, TruthChange, apply_changes

    db = _session(tmp_path, "bogus-field.db")
    try:
        item = _items(db, 1)[0]
        result = apply_changes(
            db, ParsedTruthSheet(changes=[TruthChange(
                row_number=2, item_id=item.id, field="字段不存在", label="字段不存在",
                before="unset", after="x",
            )]),
            contracts={item.id: CONTRACT}, current_truth={item.id: _truth(0)},
            actor="liangjx", edit_key="truth-import:10:bogus", reason="人工校验后回填",
        )
        assert result["summary"]["failed_items"] == 1 and result["summary"]["applied_items"] == 0
        assert not result["ignored"]
    finally:
        db.close()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_truth_kind.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from app.native_baseline_regression import compute_truth_metrics


def _level_row(status: str = "completed", predicted: str | None = "L2") -> dict:
    row: dict = {
        "status": status,
        "expected_result": {"level": "L2"},
    }
    if predicted is not None:
        row["predicted_result"] = {"level": predicted}
    return row


def test_level_truth_kind_survives_a_run_where_everything_failed() -> None:
    """一轮全失败也必须如实报告这是等级回归。

    2026-08-28 run133：250 条全失败 → level_metrics 为 null，前端只能靠它倒推
    口径，于是把灵感图的回归页显示成了「知识图谱按字段核对」。口径由基准集
    决定，与本轮跑没跑出结果无关。
    """

    metrics = compute_truth_metrics(
        [_level_row(status="failed", predicted=None) for _ in range(3)]
    )

    assert metrics["truth_kind"] == "level"
    assert metrics["level_metrics"] is None
    assert metrics["valid_predictions"] == 0


def test_level_truth_kind_holds_when_results_exist() -> None:
    metrics = compute_truth_metrics([_level_row(), _level_row(predicted="L3")])

    assert metrics["truth_kind"] == "level"
    assert metrics["level_metrics"] is not None


def test_structured_truth_kind_is_reported_for_field_baselines() -> None:
    """知识图谱那类结构化真值仍要走字段口径，不能被一并改成等级。"""

    metrics = compute_truth_metrics(
        [
            {
                "status": "completed",
                "expected_result": {"style": "现代", "material": "木"},
                "predicted_result": {"style": "现代", "material": "石"},
            }
        ]
    )

    assert metrics["truth_kind"] == "structured_fields"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_baseline_truth_revisions.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.baseline_truth import (
    BaselineTruthError,
    DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
    append_baseline_truth_revision,
    resolve_effective_baseline_truth,
)
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    BaselineTruthFrozenError,
    BaselineTruthRevision,
)


def _structured_item(db: Session) -> BaselineSetItem:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="kg-truth.jpg",
        stored_name="kg-truth.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=20,
        sha256="a" * 64,
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
    )
    baseline = BaselineSet(
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        name="知识图谱真值修订测试集",
        description="",
        default_expected_level="L1",
        fingerprint="b" * 64,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add_all([asset, baseline])
    db.flush()
    item = BaselineSetItem(
        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        expected_level=None,
        expected_result_json=canonical_json(
            {"ll_id": "ll-1", "is_single": 0, "style": ""}
        ),
        truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "asset_id": asset.id,
                "sha256": asset.sha256,
                "workflow_input": {
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
            }
        ),
    )
    db.add(item)
    db.flush()
    return item

def test_design_case_truth_revision_revalidates_terminal_contract(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'case-truth-revision.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            item = _design_case_item(db)
            original = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)

            with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthError) as invalid:
                append_baseline_truth_revision(
                    db,
                    item=item,
                    expected_level=None,
                    expected_result={
                        **original.expected_result,
                        "style": "错误根路径",
                    },
                    truth_contract_key=DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
                    workflow_input=original.workflow_input,
                    source_baseline_run_id=None,
                    source_production_run_id=None,
                    source_correction_key="case-correction-invalid",
                    reason="验证案例合同",
                    actor="ops",
                )
            assert invalid.value.code == "design_case_truth_result_invalid"

            for invalid_review in (
                {"category_one": "不存在的一级类目"},
                {"region_type": "火星"},
                {"reason": "过长" * 26},
            ):
                with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthError) as schema_invalid:
                    append_baseline_truth_revision(
                        db,
                        item=item,
                        expected_level=None,
                        expected_result={
                            "case_cloud_id": "case-1",
                            "level": "L2",
                            "case_review": invalid_review,
                        },
                        truth_contract_key=DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
                        workflow_input=original.workflow_input,
                        source_baseline_run_id=None,
                        source_production_run_id=None,
                        source_correction_key=None,
                        reason="验证严格字段合同",
                        actor="ops",
                    )
                assert schema_invalid.value.code == "design_case_truth_result_invalid"

            revision = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    **original.expected_result,
                    "case_review": {"style": "自然"},
                },
                truth_contract_key=DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
                workflow_input=original.workflow_input,
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="case-correction-valid",
                reason="运营确认案例风格",
                actor="ops",
            )

            assert json.loads(revision.expected_result_json) == {
                "case_cloud_id": "case-1",
                "case_review": {"style": "自然"},
                "level": "L2",
            }
            assert resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item).expected_result == {
                "case_cloud_id": "case-1",
                "case_review": {"style": "自然"},
                "level": "L2",
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'truth-v0.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)

                "is_single": 0,
                "style": "",
            }

                "source": "domestic",
                "is_single": 0,
                "ll_id": "ll-1",

    finally:

def test_truth_revisions_append_hash_chain_and_replay_idempotently(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'truth-chain.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            item = _structured_item(db)
            original = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)
            first = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "style": "现代简约",
                },
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                workflow_input={
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:kg:1",
                reason="运营确认风格",
                actor="ops",
            )
            replay = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "style": "现代简约",
                },
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                workflow_input={
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:kg:1",
                reason="运营确认风格",
                actor="ops",
            )

            assert replay.id == first.id
            assert first.revision == 1
            assert first.previous_hash == original.canonical_hash
            assert len(first.canonical_hash) == 64

            with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthError) as conflict:
                append_baseline_truth_revision(
                    db,
                    item=item,
                    expected_level=None,
                    expected_result={
                        "ll_id": "ll-1",
                        "is_single": 0,
                        "style": "工业",
                    },
                    truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                    workflow_input={
                        "source": "domestic",
                        "is_single": 0,
                        "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    },
                    source_baseline_run_id=None,
                    source_production_run_id=None,
                    source_correction_key="correction:kg:1",
                    reason="冲突重放",
                    actor="ops",
                )
            assert conflict.value.code == "truth_revision_idempotency_conflict"

            second = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "style": "自然",
                },
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                workflow_input={
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:kg:2",
                reason="运营再次确认风格",
                actor="ops",
            )

            assert second.revision == 2
            assert second.previous_hash == first.canonical_hash
            effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)
            assert effective.source == "revision"
            assert effective.revision == 2
            assert effective.revision_id == second.id
            assert effective.expected_result["style"] == "自然"
            assert effective.canonical_hash == second.canonical_hash
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_truth_revision_rows_cannot_be_updated_or_deleted(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'truth-immutable.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            item = _structured_item(db)
            row = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "style": "现代简约",
                },
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                workflow_input={
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:immutable:1",
                reason="运营确认",
                actor="ops",
            )
            row_id = row.id
            db.commit()

            row.reason = "不允许覆盖"
            with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthFrozenError):
                db.flush()
            db.rollback()

            persisted = db.get(BaselineTruthRevision, row_id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert json.loads(persisted.expected_result_json)["style"] == "现代简约"
            db.delete(persisted)
            with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthFrozenError):
                db.flush()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_concurrency_recovery_20260912.py =====
"""Concurrency self-throttling regressions observed on run328 (2026-09-12).

Observed: admission collapsed to 9/14/4 of 3x32 slots because the phase
timer counted executor queue wait; ~18% ServerOverloaded 429s never reached
the ramp's pressure bar; rejected waiters shared one available_at; and a lost
pool lock discarded a fully prepared request body. No provider calls here.
"""
import asyncio
import json
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW  # noqa: F401
from test_batch_control_windows import failure


def success(db, number, seconds):
    db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=number, step_attempt_id=number,
        invocation_key='root', node_instance_id='prompt', status='succeeded',
        provider='doubao', model_id='test', endpoint_id='ep-bi-other', quota_pool_id=1,
        upstream_status_code=200, reserved_input_tokens=30000, reserved_output_tokens=0,
        estimated_cost_micros=1, created_at=NOW-timedelta(seconds=300),
        finished_at=NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds), evidence_json='{}'))
    db.flush()


def _warm(db, pool, rpm):
    admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()))
    evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
    # Ramp window already open for 100 s; the next admission at NOW+100 is
    # inside the 180 s idle threshold but past the 180 s ramp window.
    evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(
        requests_per_minute=rpm, tokens_per_minute=rpm*30000,
        ramp_started_at=(NOW-timedelta(seconds=100)).timestamp())
    row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence)
    db.commit()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('rejections,expected_rpm', [
    (0, 12.0),   # healthy window keeps the 1.2x ramp
    (1, 12.0),
    (3, 12.0),   # 3/13 = 23% ServerOverloaded tracks provider load: reported, not a hold
    # 10/20 = 50% is the same signal at a different share, and on 2026-09-15 the
    # majority rule that used to cut here is what pinned production at 2 RPM: a
    # rejection lands in the window immediately, a success minutes later, so a
    # slow stream always looks like a majority. Only RequestBurstTooFast holds
    # the ramp now; see test_batch_overload_does_not_hold_ramp.
    (10, 12.0),
    (18, 12.0),  # 18/28 = 64%, still the provider's load and still not a hold
])
def test_overload_rate_is_reported_and_never_holds_the_ramp(database, rejections, expected_rpm):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # Older physical requests whose responses land after the warm admission;
        # the controller state must stay on the newest admitted row.
        for index in range(10):
            success(db, 200+index, 20+index)
        for index in range(rejections):
            failure(db, 300+index, 5+index)  # early finishes: their 5/10/20 s backoff has expired
        db.commit()
        _warm(db, pool, 10)
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=100))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(expected_rpm)
        assert state['feedback']['resource_rejections'] == rejections
        assert 'hold' not in state['feedback']
        assert state['feedback']['overload_rate'] == pytest.approx(rejections/(10+rejections), abs=1e-4)


def test_burst_protection_at_the_same_share_does_hold_the_ramp(database):
    # The contrast that makes the case above a measurement rather than a shrug.
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        for index in range(10):
            success(db, 200+index, 20+index)
        for index in range(10):
            failure(db, 300+index, 5+index, code='RequestBurstTooFast')
        db.commit()
        _warm(db, pool, 10)
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=100))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(8.0)
        assert state['feedback']['pressure'] is True


def test_overload_rate_ignored_below_minimum_sample(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        success(db, 200, 20)
        failure(db, 300, 5)  # 1/2 = 50% but only two completions
        db.commit()
        _warm(db, pool, 10)
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=100))
        assert state['feedback']['overload_rate'] == 0.0
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(12.0)


def test_rejected_waiters_get_spread_retry_inside_one_gap(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)  # 2 RPM cold start: 30 s gap
    seen = set()
    for number in range(2, 22):
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
                admit(db, pool, number, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1))
            assert caught.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
            # delay 29 s plus 0..15 s jitter (capped by the gap itself)
            assert 29 <= caught.value.retry_after_seconds <= 45
            seen.add(caught.value.retry_after_seconds)
    assert len(seen) > 1


def test_phase_peak_excludes_executor_queue_wait():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime

    async def scenario():
        def operation():
            time.sleep(.05)
            return True
            yield  # pragma: no cover - generator form
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=6, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            await asyncio.gather(*(runtime.run(operation()) for _ in range(6)))
            # Six 50 ms phases behind one thread: the last one queues for
            # roughly five phases, so queue wait dwarfs any single phase.
            assert runtime.phase_queue_peak_seconds >= 2*runtime.phase_peak_seconds
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_single_slow_preparation_does_not_halve_admission():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    policy = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=32, initial=32)
    assert policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 2.5)) == 32
    assert policy.reason == 'validated_ceiling'
    # 2026-09-19: one slow sample only holds; the second consecutive one cuts
    assert policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 9.5)) == 32
    assert policy.reason == 'phase_watch'
    assert policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 9.5)) == 16
    assert policy.reason == 'local_resource_pressure'
    for _ in range(2):
        policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 2.9))
    assert policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 2.9)) > 16


def test_async_consumers_back_off_when_nothing_is_claimable():
    from app.async_runtime_worker import consume
    attempts = []

    async def step():
        attempts.append(time.monotonic())
        return False

    async def scenario():
        end = asyncio.get_running_loop().time()+.3
        await consume(step, slots=1, should_continue=lambda: asyncio.get_running_loop().time() < end,
                      poll_seconds=.02, shutdown_seconds=.1)
    asyncio.run(scenario())
    # Fixed 20 ms polling would attempt ~15 times; backoff caps at 4x poll.
    assert 2 <= len(attempts) <= 8


def test_prepared_hold_only_for_short_submission_waits():
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import _prepared_hold_seconds
    from app.workflow_model_budget import WorkflowModelBudgetError

    def error(code='model_batch_submission_wait', wait=20, retryable=True):
        return WorkflowModelBudgetError(code, 'wait', status_code=409,
                                        retryable=retryable, retry_after_seconds=wait)
    deadline = datetime.now(timezone.utc)+timedelta(hours=1)
    kwargs = dict(transport_mode='batch', transport_deadline=deadline, held_seconds=0.0)
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), **kwargs) == 20.0
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(wait=90), **kwargs) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(code='quota_pool_cooling_down'), **kwargs) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(retryable=False), **kwargs) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(wait=True), **kwargs) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), **{**kwargs, 'held_seconds': 170.0}) is None
    soon = datetime.now(timezone.utc)+timedelta(seconds=40)
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), transport_mode='batch', transport_deadline=soon, held_seconds=0.0) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), transport_mode='batch', transport_deadline=None, held_seconds=0.0) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), transport_mode='online', transport_deadline=None, held_seconds=0.0) == 20.0


def test_large_waiting_population_is_spread_by_waiters_over_rpm(database):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission, WAIT_SPREAD_MAX_SECONDS
    from app.models import ModelQuotaPool
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)  # 2 RPM: 30 s gap
        row = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        plain, spread = set(), set()
        for _ in range(12):
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
                check_submission(db, pool=row, reserved_tokens=0, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1))
            plain.add(caught.value.retry_after_seconds)
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
                check_submission(db, pool=row, reserved_tokens=0, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1), waiting_hint=253)
            spread.add(caught.value.retry_after_seconds)
        assert max(plain) <= 45                      # delay 29 + jitter <= 15
        assert max(spread) <= 45+WAIT_SPREAD_MAX_SECONDS
        assert max(spread) > 60 and min(spread) >= 29   # 253 waiters at 2 RPM: up to +600 s
        # a single waiter never gets spread
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            check_submission(db, pool=row, reserved_tokens=0, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1), waiting_hint=1)
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds <= 45


def test_preflight_passes_waiter_count_to_controller(monkeypatch):
    from app import batch_submission
    seen = {}
    original = batch_submission.check_submission
    def spy(db, **kwargs):
        seen.update(kwargs)
        return original(db, **kwargs)
    monkeypatch.setattr(batch_submission, 'check_submission', spy)
    import inspect
    assert 'waiting_hint' in inspect.signature(original).parameters
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    src = inspect.getsource(preflight_model_pool)
    assert 'waiting_hint=int(waiting)' in src and "RuntimeDispatchItem.last_error_code == WAIT_CODE" in src

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_control_windows.py =====
"""Regressions for global collapse, online starvation and wasted image prep."""
import json
from datetime import timedelta
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
import pytest
from app.models import ModelQuotaPool, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case, _drive_without_cooldown


def failure(db, number, seconds, code='ServerOverloaded'):
    db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=number, step_attempt_id=number,
        invocation_key='root', node_instance_id='prompt', status='failed',
        provider='doubao', model_id='test', endpoint_id='ep-bi-other', quota_pool_id=1,
        error_code='model_provider_429', upstream_status_code=429,
        reserved_input_tokens=30000, reserved_output_tokens=0, estimated_cost_micros=1,
        # These are long-tail responses from older requests, not newer admissions.
        created_at=NOW-timedelta(seconds=300), finished_at=NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds),
        evidence_json=json.dumps({'failure': {'provider_error_code': code}})))
    db.flush()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('code,expected_rpm,expected_tpm', [
    ('RequestBurstTooFast', 12, 360000),
    ('ServerOverloaded', 2, 60000),
], ids=['burst-backoff-is-not-idle', 'overload-has-no-backoff-to-wait-out'])
def test_resource_backoff_is_not_a_cold_idle_restart(database, code, expected_rpm, expected_tpm):
    """Replay the observed 240 s admission gap with late 429s throughout it.

    Burst rejections still carry the 5/10/20/40/60 s chain, so the gap is spent
    waiting them out: it must not read as idleness and drop the rate to the
    2 RPM start; the window ends one cut down (pressure), not restarted.

    Since 2026-09-17 ServerOverloaded carries no shared backoff at all, so a
    240 s gap with only overloads landing in it is genuine idleness and the
    ordinary cold restart applies - nothing was holding the pool shut.
    """
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # Insert older physical requests first; only their finishes are late.
        for index, second in enumerate((30, 60, 95, 115, 151, 175)):
            failure(db, 100 + index, second, code=code)
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()))
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(
            requests_per_minute=15, tokens_per_minute=450000)
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence)
        db.commit()
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW + timedelta(seconds=240))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(expected_rpm)
        assert state['tokens_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(expected_tpm)
        # Resource wait buys no burst credits or skipped ramp stages.
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            admit(db, pool, 3, NOW + timedelta(seconds=240))
        later = admit(db, pool, 4, NOW + timedelta(hours=6))
        assert later['requests_per_minute'] <= 2


def test_many_429_cooldowns_in_one_window_cannot_repeatedly_halve_pool(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        p = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        p.cooldown_reason = 'provider_rate_limited'
        p.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
        db.commit()
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=60))
        p.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=120)
        db.commit()
        next_state = admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(seconds=120))
        assert next_state['requests_per_minute'] == state['requests_per_minute']
        assert next_state['tokens_per_minute'] == state['tokens_per_minute']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('hint', [None, 30], ids=['no-hint-no-wait', 'vendor-hint-only'])
def test_late_overload_waits_only_its_vendor_hint_without_resetting_or_halving_healthy_pacing(database, hint):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        failure(db, 100, 40)
        if hint is not None:
            row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            row.evidence_json = json.dumps({'failure': {'provider_error_code': 'ServerOverloaded', 'retry_after_seconds': hint}})
        state = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        db.commit()
        if hint is None:
            # Since 2026-09-17 an overload carries no shared backoff of its own.
            assert check_submission(db, pool=db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool), reserved_tokens=30000,
                now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=41))
        else:
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
                check_submission(db, pool=db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool), reserved_tokens=30000,
                    now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=41))
            assert exc.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
            assert exc.value.retry_after_seconds == 29
        next_state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=71))
        assert next_state['requests_per_minute'] == state['requests_per_minute']
        assert next_state['tokens_per_minute'] == state['tokens_per_minute']
